# Phase-7 Wave 7 industry-specific repository joins — P7-INDUSTRY-JOINS-W7

## Scope and evidence boundary

This bounded tranche records page-6 unauthenticated public GitHub Search API repository metadata for all 17 industries and five discovery buckets. Waves 1–6 identities were excluded case-insensitively; Wave-7 duplicates were excluded; no quota was padded. This is not an overall Phase-7 completion claim.

- Industries: 17; discovery buckets: 85; per-bucket ceiling: 20; target positions: 1,700.
- Wave-7 page-6 join records: 833; selected metadata-level joins: 485; held/unknown: 334; rejected: 14.
- Underfilled buckets: 50/85; quota-reached buckets: 35; no bucket was padded or forced.
- Unique canonical identities and records: 833 / 833; Waves 1–6 identity overlap: 0; Wave-7 duplicate records: 0.
- Discovery: 85 unauthenticated GitHub Search API page-6 queries, 93 HTTP attempts, 8 rate-limit reset events preserved; repository pages/README/source were not fetched.
- Every row is reference-only, rights uncleared, SBOM unknown/not scanned, execution UNEXECUTED, and admission NOT_ADMITTED.

## Quality gaps, contradictions, falsifiers and next gates

- GitHub Search API metadata is first-party public metadata evidence only. Search result descriptions and counters do not establish repository capability, adoption, production fitness, rights clearance, or implementation readiness.
- A declared SPDX license signal remains rights uncleared; NOASSERTION remains unknown. Sponsorship, contributor provenance, NOTICE obligations, dependency manifests and SBOM are unknown because no repository page, README, source or manifest was fetched.
- Archived/fork metadata is retained as reject; generic/thin/unresolved matches are held. Contradictions remain on each JSONL record and are not silently resolved.
- Falsifiers and next read-only clean-room gates remain per join row. Any future gate must stop before clone, source copy, execution, build, deployment, scan, benchmark or admission.
- Fresh CENA callback delivery is verified in herdr-2 pane w659e02f80e5bb1-1 after the required 2-second readback; this lane does not claim overall Phase-7 completion.

## Boundary and verification contract

research_only=true; implementation_authorized=false; execution_status=UNEXECUTED; admission_status=NOT_ADMITTED; admitted_blocks=0; client/private data false; no login or credentials; no clone/copy/source execution/build/deploy/benchmark/scan; shared Phase-7 state unpromoted.
The lane smoke must recompute JSONL schema, exact counts, 17-industry/85-bucket coverage, prior-wave overlap, duplicate identity, source URL/date/HTTP/rate-limit evidence, rights/SBOM boundary, Markdown links and scoped git diff before callback.
